<a class="anchor" id="take-modern-approach"></a>

<div class="wpb_text_column wpb_content_element">
<div class="wpb_wrapper">
<h2>Take a modern approach to customer feedback.</h2>
</div>
</div>
<div class="wpb_text_column wpb_content_element">
<div class="wpb_wrapper">
<ul>
<li>Focus on critical journey points that make or break customer loyalty.</li>
<li>Free your customers to tell you what is important, in their own words.</li>
<li>Never miss a trend.</li>
<li>Hear from more customers and follow up in real-time.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div class="vc_btn3-container middle vc_btn3-left" id="learn-more-cx"><a class="vc_general vc_btn3 vc_btn3-size-md vc_btn3-shape-rounded vc_btn3-style-custom" href="https://www.wootric.com/customer-experience-management/" style="background-color:#8ac154; color:#ffffff;" title="">LEARN MORE ABOUT CX SOFTWARE</a></div><div class="vc_row wpb_row vc_inner vc_row-fluid vc_custom_1462322691823 vc_column-gap-20 boomapps_vcrow"><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12 boomapps_vccolumn"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper">
<div class="wpb_text_column wpb_content_element quote-small-blk">
<div class="wpb_wrapper">
<p style="text-align: left;"><em>“With Wootric, we quantify customer experience at key journey points using NPS, CSAT and CES. As a result, we see the impact of CX on our business and to prioritize projects more effectively.”</em></p>
<p style="text-align: left;"><strong>Amy Etheridge</strong><br/>
<em>Head of Customer Advocacy, MindTouch</em></p>

<script type="text/javascript">
<!-- WOOTRIC AD TRACK -->
    function getParameterByName(name, url) {
      if (!url) url = window.location.href;
      name = name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, "\\$&");
      var regex = new RegExp("[?&]" + name + "(=([^&#]*)|&|#|$)"),
        results = regex.exec(url);
      if (!results) return null;
      if (!results[2]) return '';
      return decodeURIComponent(results[2].replace(/\+/g, " "));
    }

    function wootricAdTracker() {
      var d = new Date();
      var ad_data = {
        gclid: getParameterByName('gclid'),
        ch: getParameterByName('ch'),
        chdtl: getParameterByName('chdtl'),
        cmp: getParameterByName('cmp'),
        keyword: getParameterByName('keyword'),
        ofrdtl: getParameterByName('ofrdtl'),
        matchtype: getParameterByName('matchtype')
      };

      d.setTime(d.getTime() + (31*24*60*60*1000));
      var expires = "expires="+ d.toUTCString();

      document.cookie = "wootric_ad_data=" + JSON.stringify(ad_data) +'; domain=.wootric.com;' + expires + "; path=/";
    }
    wootricAdTracker();
<!-- end of ad track code -->
